2025-06-14T22:12:17.3093954Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3094123Z <pr_title>
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3094774Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3095508Z </pr_title>
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3095684Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3095794Z <pr_description>
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3096635Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3097297Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3097378Z ## Problem
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3097674Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3098051Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3098299Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3098543Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3098802Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3099051Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3099265Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3099383Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3099783Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3100278Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3100349Z ## Solution
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3100454Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3100545Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3101015Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3101316Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3101768Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3102791Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3103399Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3103682Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3103775Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3104046Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3104231Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3104313Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3104521Z ```bash
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3105003Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3105536Z ```
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3105637Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3105724Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3105944Z ```bash
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3106554Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3107238Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3107450Z ```
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3107542Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3107679Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3107905Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3107995Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3108520Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3108973Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3109045Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3109279Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3109717Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3110175Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3110751Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3111374Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3111654Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3111993Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3112413Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3112482Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3112589Z 
2025-06-14T22:12:17.3112593Z

2025-06-14T22:14:55.9144134Z     4.
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9144422Z     5.## Problem
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9144719Z     6.
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9145263Z     7.Prefect 3.3.4 depends on `pendulum>=3.0.0,<4` for date/time operations. However, pendulum:
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9145906Z     8.
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9146552Z     9.1. **Uses Rust compilation**: Pendulum 3.x is built with maturin and includes Rust extensions for performance
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9147854Z     10.2. **ARM compilation challenges**: Building pendulum for ARM7HF (OT-2 architecture) is complex due to Rust cross-compilation requirements
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9149468Z     11.3. **Import failures**: Even when hatchling and other dependencies are resolved, missing pendulum causes Prefect import failures
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9150333Z     12.
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9150630Z     13.## Solution
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9151111Z     14.
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9151528Z     15.This repository now provides **two pendulum wheel options**:
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9152068Z     16.
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9152461Z     17.### 1. Universal Wheel (Recommended for compatibility)
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9153098Z     18.- **File**: `pendulum-3.1.0-py3-none-any.whl` (109KB)
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9153638Z     19.- **Architecture**: Platform-independent, pure Python fallback
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9154171Z     20.- **Compatibility**: Works on any Python 3.9+ system including OT-2
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9154752Z     21.- **Performance**: Slightly slower than compiled version but fully functional
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9155102Z     22.
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9155325Z     23.### 2. ARM64 Optimized Wheel (Best performance)
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9156127Z     24.- **File**: `pendulum-3.1.0-cp310-cp310-manylinux_2_17_aarch64.manylinux2014_aarch64.whl` (336KB)
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9156889Z     25.- **Architecture**: Compiled for ARM64/aarch64 systems
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9157956Z     26.- **Compatibility**: May work on OT-2 depending on glibc version and ABI compatibility
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9158783Z     27.- **Performance**: Full native performance with Rust optimizations
2025-06-14T22:14:55.9159364Z     28.

2025-06-14T22:17:51.5655233Z       ## Basic Usage Example
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5655441Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5655780Z       Once installed, you can use Prefect in your OT-2 scripts:
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5656086Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5656272Z       ```python
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5656611Z       from prefect import flow, task
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5656984Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5657233Z       @task
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5657513Z       def prepare_plate():
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5657891Z           print("Preparing plate...")
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5658376Z           # Your OT-2 plate preparation code here
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5658830Z           return "Plate ready"
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5659193Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5659445Z       @task  
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5659757Z       def run_protocol(plate_status):
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5660439Z           print(f"Running protocol with {plate_status}")
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5661104Z           # Your OT-2 protocol code here
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5661554Z           return "Protocol completed"
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5661939Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5662204Z       @flow
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5662498Z       def ot2_workflow():
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5662859Z           plate = prepare_plate()
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5663262Z           result = run_protocol(plate)
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5663703Z           print(f"Workflow finished: {result}")
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5664130Z           return result
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5664444Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5664718Z       # Run the workflow
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5665065Z       if __name__ == "__main__":
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5665431Z           ot2_workflow()
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5665755Z       ```

2025-06-14T22:17:51.5684811Z

2025-06-14T22:17:51.5685474Z

2025-06-14T22:17:51.5686364Z          ```python

2025-06-14T22:17:51.5687011Z          ```

2025-06-14T22:17:51.5687944Z          ```python

2025-06-14T22:17:51.5704513Z       ## System Requirements
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5704860Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5705175Z       - **Architecture**: ARMv7l (Raspberry Pi 3B+)
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5705632Z       - **Python**: 3.10+
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5705999Z       - **OS**: Opentrons custom firmware
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5706642Z       - **Network**: Internet access for initial download
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5707162Z       - **Storage**: ~20MB free space for all wheels
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5707583Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5707844Z       ## Background
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5708133Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5708663Z       This solution addresses the complexity of installing Prefect on OT-2 systems where:
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5709305Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5709713Z       - Standard pip install fails due to compilation requirements
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5710358Z       - pendulum (Prefect dependency) requires Rust compilation 
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5711082Z       - ARMv7l wheels are not readily available
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5711597Z       - Limited system tools (no git, bash, venv)
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5712027Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5712625Z       The pre-built wheels in this repository eliminate these issues by providing compatible binaries.
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5713319Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5713583Z       ## Support
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5713867Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5714146Z       If you encounter issues:
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5714495Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5714822Z       1. Check that you're on an ARMv7l system (OT-2)
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5715288Z       2. Verify Python 3.10 is being used
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5715740Z       3. Ensure network connectivity to GitHub
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5716268Z       4. Try the automated installer first before manual methods
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5716743Z       
2025-06-14T22:17:51.5717183Z       For additional help, refer to the repository issues or documentation.
